# Graph Report - SEMD  (2026-07-01)

## Corpus Check
- 1832 files · ~2,129,271 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 32601 nodes · 36459 edges · 2254 communities (2064 shown, 190 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 611 edges (avg confidence: 0.63)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `370d825c`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
